S.O. 4727,—-Whereas Messrs Sunil Syn-Chen? Limited, 
17/18, Old. Industrial Area, Alwar, Rajasihun (RJ/201U) 
(hereinafter referred to as the said establishment ) have applied 
for exemption under sub-section (2AJ of Section 17 ot the 
Employees’ Frovulent Funds fifth Miscellaneous ITovis.ons 
Aci, i'J52 (ly of 1952) (hereinafter leferrcd to as the said 
Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
lhe employees of the said establishment are, without malt¬ 
ing any separate contribution or payment .of premium, in 
enjoyment of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme 
of the Lite Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life Insurance wtiich are more favourable to such em¬ 
ployees than the benefits admissible under the Employees’ 
Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter refer- 
icd to as the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to the conditions specified in the Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

1, The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Com- 
mitsioner, Rajasthan and mainunn such accounts and pro¬ 
vide such facilities for inspection, as the Central Govern¬ 
ment muy direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government may, from time to time, direct 
under clause in) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the 
said Act, within 15 days from the close of every month, 

_ 3. A'l expenses involved ip the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of Insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts, payment of Jnspcct'on charges etc. shall bv 
borne by the employer. 

■>, The employer shrill display on the Notice Hoard of 
the establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees. 

5. Wherea- an employee, who is alre idv a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act. is employed In 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Dreminm in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India, 

A "Hie employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 


29, 1984 /ffW h, I9tl£ 4537 


Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployers under the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
ucnefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more Favourable to tne employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than th; amount that 
would be payable had employee been covered under the 
said Scheme, the employer shall pay lhe difference to the 
legal heir/nominee of the employee as compensation, 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Rajasthan and 
where any amendment is likely to affect adversely the in¬ 
terest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, _nive a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of 
view. 

9. W'hcie. for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme” 'are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life 
Insurance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the .employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
r rompt payment of the sum assured to the nominee/Legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in any 
case within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
in all respects”.

S-O. 4728,—Whereas the M/«- Karur Munieipsl Electrical 
Undertaking Karur, Trivhy, QN/3426) hai applied lor exemp¬ 
tion from Employees’ family Pension Scheme, 1971 under 
siib-seCtion (1A) of section 17 of the Employee.-/ Provident 
Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, lj *52 (19 of 1952), 

And whereas, in the opinion of the Central Government 
the benefits in the nature of Family Pension under the Cen¬ 
tral Government Family Pension Scheme, 1964 and applicable 
to the employees of (he said establishment are Dot less favour¬ 
able than the benefits provided under (he Said Aci, and 
the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971, 

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the said Act, and subject 
to the conditions specified hereunder, the Central Government 
hereby exempts the said establishment from the operation of 
all provisions of the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 
1971 for a period of three years. 

CONDITIONS 

(1) Notwithstanding anything contained in the Family 
Pension Scheme of the establishmentn if the amount 


of pension payable in respect of any member upon 
his death is less than the amount ot family Pension 
payable if he were a member of the Employees’ 
family Pension Scheme, 1971 the Employer shall 
sanction the Family Pension , v/niefc is admissible 
under the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971. 

(2) The employer shall maintain such accounts, submit 

such returns und provide for sum facility for ins¬ 
pection as the Central Government may fiom time 
to time direct. 

(3) AU expenses involved in ihc admim'-traiion of the 
Family Pension Scheme of the said establishment 
including maintenance o; accounts, submission of 
accounts and return, transfer ot accounts, shall be 
borne by the employer. 

(4) Die employer shall display on the notice board of 
the establishment a copy of the rules incorporating 
therein all amendments, if any of the Family Pension 
.Scheme of the said establishment as a approved by 
the Central Government alongwith a translation of 
the salient features thereof in language understood 
by the majority of the employees, 

(5) No amendment of the rules of the Family Pension 

Scheme of the establishments adversely allccting the 
interests of the employees shall be made without 
the prior approval of the Central Government in 
the Ministry of Labour and the Central Provident 
Fund Commissioner. The Central Government and 
the Central Provident Fund Commissioner will, 
before giving therein approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employee.-,' to explain their point 
of view.

plied for exemption from Employees’ Family Pension Scnerac, 
1971, under sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the Employees' 
Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 
of 1952). 

And whereas, in the opinion of the Central Government 
the benefits in the nature of Family Pension under the 
Central Government Family Pension Scheme, 1964 and appli¬ 
cable to the employees or the said establi:hment are not lets 
favourable than the benefits provided under the said Act, 
and the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971. 

Now, therefore in exercise of the powery conferred by 
sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the said Act, and subject 
to the conditions specified hereunder, the Central Govern¬ 
ment hereby exempts the said establishment from the opera¬ 
tion of jdl provisions of the Employeeu’ Family Pension 
Scheme, 1971 for a period of three years,

High Court Buildings, Mauras-600104, (TN/8766) has ap¬ 
plied for exemption from Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 
1971, under sjiD-scclion (1A) of section 17 o fthe Emp¬ 
loyees’ Provident Funds and MisceUanoous Provisions Act, 
1952 (19 of 1952); 

And, whereas, ■ in the opinion of the Central Government 
the benefits in the nature of Family Pension under the Cen¬ 
tral Government Family Pension Scheme, 1964 and applicable 
to the employees of the said establishment are not less favour¬ 
able than the benefits provided under the said Act, and the 
Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971; 

Now, therefore, In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the said Act, and subject 
to the conditions specified hereunder, the Central Govern¬ 
ment hereby exempts the said establishment from the opera¬ 
tion of all provisions of the Employees’ Family Pension 
Scheme, 1971 for a period of three years, 

CONDITIONS. 

(1) Notwithstanding anything contained in the Family 
Pension Scheme of the establishments if the amount 
of pension payable in respect of any member upon 
his death is less than the amount of family Pension 
payable if. he were a member of the Employees’ 
Family Pension Scheme, 3971 the Employer shall 
sanction Family Pension which is admissible under 

» Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971. 

(2) The employer shall maintain such accounts, submit 

such returns and provide for such facility for ins¬ 
pection as the Central Government may from time 
to time direct. 

(3) All expenses involved in the administration of the 

Family Pension Scheme of the said establishment 
including maintenance of accounts, submission of 
accounts and return, transfer of accounts, shall bo 
borne by the employer. 

(4) The employer shall display on the notice board of 
the establishment a copy of the rules incorporating 
therein .all amendments, if any of the FnmUv Pen¬ 
sion Scheme of the said establishment as approved 
by the Central Government alongwlth a translation 
of the salient features thereof in language understood 
by the majority of the employees. 

(5) No amendment of the rules of the Family Pension 

Scheme qf the establishments adversely affecting the 
interests of the employees shall be made without 
the prior approval of the Central Government in 
the Ministry of Labour and the Central Provident 
Fund Commissioner. 1 The Central Government and 
the Central Provident Fund Commissioner will, 
before .giving therein approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point 
of view.

3.0. 4739.—Whereas M/*, Photo Zince Prew, Central 
Survey Office, Madrai-5 (TN/887) ha* applied for exemption 
from Employees' Family Pension Scheme, 1971, under sub¬ 
section (E) of section 17 of the Employees’ Provident Fund* 
and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 of 1952); 

And whereas, in the opinion of the Central Government 
the benefits in the nature Of Family Pension under the 
Central Government Family Pension Scheme, 1964 and appli¬ 
cable to the employees of the said establishment are not less 
favourable than the benefits provided under the said Act, 
and the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the said Act, and subject 
to the conditions specified hereunder, the Central Govern¬ 
ment hereby exempts the said establishment from the opera¬ 
tion of al provisions of 1 the Employees’ Family Pension 
Scheme, In) for a period of three years. 

CONDITIONS : 

(1) Notwithstanding anything contained in the Family 
Pension Scheme of the establishments if the amount 
of pension payable in respect of any member upon 
his death is less than the amount of Family Pension 
payable if he were a member of the Employees’ 
Family Pension Scheme, 1971 the Employer shall 
sanction the family Pension which is admissible under 
the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971. 

(2) The employer shall maintain such accounts, submit 

such returns and provide for such facility for ins¬ 
pection as the Central Government may from time 
to time direct. 

(3) All expenses involved In the administration of the 

Family Pension Scheme of the said establishment 
including maintenance of accounts, submission of 
accounts and return, transfer of accounts, shall be 
borne by the employer. 

(4) The employer shall display on the notice board of 

the establishment a copy of the rules Incorporating 
therein all amendments, if any of the Family Pen¬ 
sion Scheme of tho said establishment as approved 
by the Central Government alongwith a translation 
of the salient features thereof in language understood 
by the majority of the employees. 

(5) No amendment of the rules of the Family Pension 

Scheme of the establishments adversely affecting 
the Interest* of the employees shall be made without 
the prior approval of the Central Government in 
the Ministry of Labour and the Central Provident 
Fund Commissioner. The Central Government and 
the Central Provident Fund Commissioner will, 
before giving therein approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point 
of view.

3.0. 4741.—Whereas the M/s. Government Branch Press 
Araikar Estate Chvola Madras, (TN/8765) has applied for 
exemption from Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971, 
under sub-section (1A), of section 17 of the Employees’ 
Provident Funds' and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 
of 1952); ' , 

And whereas, in the opinion .of the Central Government 
the benefits in the nature of Family Pension under the Cen¬ 
tral Government Family Pension Scheme, 1964 and appli¬ 
cable to the employee* of the said establishment are not 
less favourable' than the benefits provided under the said Act, 
and the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971; 


Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1A) of section 17 of the said Act, and subject 
to the conditions specified hereunder, the Central Govern¬ 
ment hereby exempts the said establishment from the opera¬ 
tion of all provisions of the Employees’ Family Pension 
Scheme, 1971 for a period of three years. 

CONDITIONS : 

(1) Notwithstanding anything contained in the Family 
Pension Scheme of the establishments if the amount 
of pension payable in respect of any member upon 
his death is less than the amount of Family Pension 
payable if he were a member of the Employees’ 
Family Pension Scheme, 1971 the Employer shall 
sanction the Family Pension which is admissible 
under the Employees’ Family Pension Scheme, 1971. 

(2) The employer shall maintain such accounts, submit 

such returns and provide for such facility for ins¬ 
pection as the Central Governme r may from timo 
to time direct. 

(3) All expenses involved in the adra nistration of the 

Family Pension Scheme of the s lid establishment 
including maintenance .of accounts, submission of 
accounts and reutm, transfer of accounts, shall be 
borne by the employer. 

(4) The employer shall display on the notice board of 

the establishment a copy of the rules incorporating 
therein all amendments, if any of the Family Pen¬ 
sion Sfcheme of the said establishment as a approved 
by the Central Government alongwlth a translation 
of ' the salient features thereof in langur ~c under¬ 
stood by the majority of the employees. 

(5) No amendment of the rules of the Family Pension 

Scheme of the establishments adversely affecting 
the interests of the emoloyces shall be made with¬ 
out tfie prior approvd. of the Central Government 
in the Ministry of Labour and the Central Provident 
Fund Commission. The Central Government and 
the Central Provident Fund Commissioner will, be¬ 
fore giving therein approval, give a t reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point 
of view.

New Delhi, the 13th December, 1984 

S.O, 4743.—Whereas Messrs Jaipur 70a Dbgdha Utpadak 
Schkarl Sangh Limited, Jaipur (R.I /2348) (hereinafter referred 
to as the said establishment) have annlied for exemption 
under sub-section (2A) of Section 1.7 of the Employees’ Pro¬ 
vident Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 of 
1952) (hereinafter referred to as the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
the employees of the said establishment are, without mak¬ 
ing any separate contribution or payment of premium, in 
enjoyment of benefits under the C*roup Insurance Scheme 
of the Life Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life Insurance which are tfnare favourable tq such em¬ 
ployees than the benefits admissible under the Employees' 
Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, i976 (hereinafter refer- 
led to as the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to the conditions specified in the Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

T The employer in relatio’n to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the .Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner, Rajasthan and maintain such accounts and pro¬ 
vide such facilities for inspection, as the Central Govern¬ 
ment may direct from time to time. 



[ra>r M—w»i 3 (ii)]

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government may, from time to time, direct 
under clause (a) of sub-section (3A) ot section 17 qf the 
said Act, within 15 days from the close of every month.

3. All expenses involved in the administruljou of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment _Qf insurance premia, trans- 
ter of accounts, payment of inspection charges otc, shall be 
borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shaJl display on til* Notice Roard of 
the estublislument, a copy of the rules ot the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees' Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment. th e employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in lespect of him to the The Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group insurance 
Scheme appropriately, if tne benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees under the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance, if on the death of an employee the amount pay¬ 
able under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered tinder the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
hcir/nomince of the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Fajasthan and 
where any amendment is likely to affect adversely the in¬ 
terest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of 
view. 

9. Where, lor any reason, ihe employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the-Lil’c Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. v.ithin the due date, as fixed by tne Life 
insurance Corporation of India, and (he policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11 . In case of default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme "but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the mettrbery covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
nrompt pavmcnt of the sum assured to the nominee /Legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in any 
case within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
ip all respects”.

S.O. 4744.—Whereas Messrs Marwar Gramin Bank Pali 
(RJ/2595) (hereinafter referred to as (he said establishment) 
have applied for exemption \mdcr sub-section (2A) of Sec¬ 
tion 17 of the Employees’ Provident Funds and Miscellaneous 
Provisions Act, 1952 (19 of 19J2) (hereinafter referred to 
as the .said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
the employees of the said establishment are, without mak¬ 
ing any separate contribution or payment of premium, in 
enjoyment of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme 


of the Life Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life Insurance which arc inore favourable to such em¬ 
ployees than the benefits admissible under the Employees’ 
Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter refer¬ 
red to as the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to (he conditions specified in the Schedule aimexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

J. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner, Rajasthan and maintain such accounts and pro¬ 
vide such facilities for inspection, as the Central Govern¬ 
ment may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government' may, from time to time, direct 
under clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the 
said Act, within 15 days from the close of every month- 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be 
borne by the employer, 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of 
the establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees' Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act. is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the cm- 

loyees under the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
eneflts available under the Group Insurance Scheme arc 
more favourable to the employees than (he benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance, if on the death of an employee t]j,c amount pay¬ 
able under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
beir/nomince of the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendnjent of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 

ance Scheme, shall he made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Rajasthan and 
where any amendment is likely Jo affect adversely the in¬ 
terest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of 
view. . 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this .Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
Ihe exemption shall be liable to be cancelled, 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
nremiimV etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life 
Insurance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, Ihe exemption is liable to be cancelled, 

11. In case of default, if a3V made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer.

prompt payment of the sum assured to the nominee /Legal

case within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
in all respects”.

S.O. 4745.—Whereas Messrs Hindustan Automobiles Sail 
kur Chandra Road, Jaipur (RJ/1167) (hereinafter referred to 
as the said establishment j have applied for exemption under 
»ub,-aeetion (2A) ol‘ Section 17 of the Employees’ provident 
bunds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 of 1952} 
(hereinafter referred to as the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is sutislied that 
the employees of tl said establishment are, without mus¬ 
ing any separate cc nbutidn or payment of premium, in 
enjoyment ol benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme 
of the Lite Insurance Corporation of India ill the nature of 
lifo Insurance which are more favourable to such em¬ 
ployees than the benefits admissible unjer the Employees’ 
deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinniter reter- 
icd to as the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, • iu exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to the conditions specified in the Schedule 'annexed hereto, 
the Central .Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

■ SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident fund Com¬ 
missioner. Rajasthan and maintain such accounts and pro¬ 
vide such facilities for inspection, as the Central Govern* 
may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government may, from time to tunc, direct 
under clause (a)' of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the 
said Act, within 15 days from the cloje of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts; payment of inspection charges etc. shall be 
borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of 
the establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith'a translation of the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees' Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6. The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the' gm- 

loyecs under the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
cnefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble mtder the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, if on. the death of an employee the amount pay¬ 
able under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir/nominee of the employee as compensation. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be. made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fue l Commissioner, Rajasthan and 
where any amendment is likely to affect adversely the in¬ 
terest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable 


opportunity to the employees to explain their poiht o) 

view, 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner 
Ihe exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where,' for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
piemium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life 
Jnsutanec Corporation of India, upd tho policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption is liable to be .cancelled. 

11. In caso of default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer, ■ 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt payment of the sum‘assured to Ihe nominee /Legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it afnd'in any 
case within one morph from the receipt of claim complete 
in all respects”.

S.O, 4746,—Whereas Messrs Stepwel Industrial Limited, 
19*B, Industrial Area, ph^se-T, Maya Puri, New Delhi-110064 
(DL/3826) (hereinafter referred to a» the said establishment) 
have applied for exemption under sub-section (2A) of Sec¬ 
tion 17 of the Employees’ Provident Funds and Miscellane¬ 
ous Provisions Act, 1952, (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred 
to as the said Act)) 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
the employees of the said establishment are, without making 
any separate contribution or payment of premium, in en¬ 
joyment of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme of 
the Life Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life Insurance which are more Favourable to such employees 
than the benefits admissible tinder the Employees’ Deposit 
Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter referred to as 
the said Scheme); 

Now, theiefore.- in exercise of the power:, conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and spbject 
to the conditions specified in Jhe Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts' the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation , of all the provisions-of .the said 
Scheme for a period of three years, 

SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident -Fund Com¬ 
missioner, Delhi and maintain such accounls and provide 
such facilities for inspection, as the Central Government 
mav .direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of,the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every mbftih. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc, .shall 
be borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of 
the' establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group 'Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the -Central Government and 
ns and when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient 
features thereof, in the- language of the majority of the 
f mployees, 

5. Whereas an employee, who is. already a .member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted 'under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group, Insurance Scheme and nay nece 
.sary -premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor 
poration of India, 

6. The employer sha'l arrange to enhance the benefi 
available to tb c employees under the Group Insurant

Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees under the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
nenefits available Under the Group Insurance Scheme arc 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount pay¬ 
able under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be- payable had employee beep covered under the sakl 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir/nominee of the employee as compensation. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insurance 
Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of the 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner. Delhi and where 
any amendment Is likely to affect adversely the interest of 
the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
shall before giving bis approval, give a reasonable oppor¬ 
tunity to the employees to explain their point of view. 

9. Where, for any reason, the employes of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life 
insurance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if" any made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
rissurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer, 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the T.ife Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt pavment of the sum assured to the nominee /Legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in any 
case within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
In all respects”.

S.O 4748.—Whereas Messrs Chinar Exports Private 
Limited, 101-A, Surya Kiran Building, 19, Kasturba Gandhi 
Marg. New Delhi-110001 (DL/4965) (hereinatter referred to 
as the said establishment) have applied for o\cnip : ion under 
sub-section (2A) of Section 17 of the 1 mployec- Provident 
Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act. 1952 (19 of 1952 1 
(hereinafter referred to as the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
the employees of the said establishment are, without making 
any separate contribution or payment of premium, in en¬ 
joyment of benefits under the Group r nsuiance Scheme' of 
the T.Te Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life insurance which are more favourable to such emnloyees 
than the benefits admissible under the Employees Deposit 





[het Il—trr 3(ii)] 


STITT 77 TT-tReT: feTHIT 29, 1984/Tta 8, 1906 


4561 


Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter referred to as 
the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to the conditions specified in the Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner, Delhi and maintain such accounts and piov.d . 
such facilities for inspection, as the Central Government 
may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection chatges as 
the Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the said Act. 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall 
be borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of 
the establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient 
le." lutes thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees.' 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already, a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act. is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a-member 'of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of h ; m to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees wider the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance scheme, if on the death .of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee Been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir'nominee of the employee as compensation. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insurance 

Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of the 
Regional Provident Fund C'-—. D.lhi and where 
any amendment is Fkely t ".v ' the interest of 

1 be employees.^ the Region * 1*0 .. ■ : ■ ■ Commissioner 

shall before giving his approval., give a reasonable ppnor- 
tun’ry to the employees to explain their point of view. 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab- 
IMitn.-pt do not remain covered under the Groun Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits la the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10 Where, for any reason, the employe- fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed, by the Life 
Trsw.-an -e Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lun-e the exemption is liable to "be cancelled. 

1 * In case of default. If any made by the employer in 
pro c ’nl of orem'um and responsibility for payment of 
a'--’ Tmc« benefits to the nominees or Tic legal heirs of 
eft":' members who would have been covered under fiv 
s n 'd firher!. Hn fo,- grant o f this exemption, shall be thal 

the employer. 

12. Upon the death of (he members covered under the 
S'cheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure

prompt payment of the sum assured to the nominee/Legal 
heirs of the' deceased member entitled tor it and in any 
ease within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
in all respects”.

S.O. 4750.—Whereas Messrs The Universal Supply Cor¬ 
poration, Sogani Bhawan, M. 1. Road, Jaipur-302001 (RJ/ 
H46) thereinafter referred to as the said establishment) have 
applied for exemption under sub-section (2A) of Section 17 
of the Employees’ Frovident Funds and Miscellaneous Pro¬ 
visions Act,-1952 (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred to as 
the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that 
the employees ol the said establishment are, without making 
any separate contribution or payment of premium, in en¬ 
joyment of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme of 
the Life Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of 
Life Insurance which are more favourable to such employee 1 -’ 
than the benefits admissible under the Employees' Deposit 
Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter referred to as 
the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the power.; conferred by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act md subject 
to the conditions specified in the Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of the said 
Scheme for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Frovident Fund Com¬ 
missioner, Rajaslhan and maintain such accounts and provide 
such facilities for inspection, as (he Central Govemmenl 
may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges ns 
the Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
chime (a) of sub-scction (3 A) of section 17 of I he said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in Ihc administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
‘(.’'omission of returns, payment of insurance premia, trans¬ 
fer of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall 
be borne by the employer. 

4. The ..’mnlnvrr shall d'S'bt-.- on ih,- Nnri-.-e Boaid of 
the cstahlrhiyifnl. a copy of th; rules >- c the Croup Insur¬ 
ance Scheme ns approved bv the Central Government and 
rs and when amended alonewitb a transhiuon of the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees, 

5. Whereas an cmplovec, who is alrendv a member of the 
Emnl-'yeeV Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, "is employed in 
his estnbh-hmmt, the r-moloyer shall immediatclv enrol him 
ns a mcmhei of the Group Insurance Scheme and puv nccex 


sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the. employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately, if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees under the said Scheme' are enhanced, so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme arc 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admissi¬ 
ble under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered under the saio 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir/nomineo of the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Rajasthan and 
where any amendment is likely to affect adversely the in¬ 
terest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to Iho employees to explain their point of 
view. 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said estiiblishftfeni, or tpe benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the etatmption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life 
Insurance Corporation of Tndia, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption Is "liable to be cancelled. 

11 . In ca;e If default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
i rompt payment of the sum assured to the nominee /Legal 
heirs iff the deceased member enfitled for it and in any 
c.w- within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
in all respects”.

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment-of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be 
borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of 
the establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme as approved by the Central Government and 
as and when amended, alongwith a translation o the salient 
features thereof, in the language of the majority of the 
employees. 


5. Whereas an employee, who ij already a member of 
the Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits avail¬ 
able to the employees under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately, if the benefits available to the employees under 
the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the benefits available 
under the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employees than the benefits admissible under the said 
Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than the amount that 
would be payable bad employee been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir/nominee pf the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner. Delhi and where 
any amendment is likely to affect adversely the interest of 
the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable oppor¬ 
tunity to the employees to explain their point of view. 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 


|>TI4 II—3(ii)]* 


• 4567 


«rror jet 73444 : fatrmr 


Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme arc reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium eto, within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insur¬ 
ance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of assur¬ 
ance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of deceased 
members who would have been covered under the said Scheme 
but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of the emp¬ 
loyer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt payment of the sum assuied to the nominee/legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in apy case 
within one month from the receipt of claim complete in all 
respects.

S.O. 4754.—Whereas Messrs Plastic and Synthetic- Pro¬ 
ducts Limited, 19-B, Industrial Area, Phasc-I, Maya Puri, 
New Delbi-64 (DL/6740) (hereinafter referred to as the said 
establishment) have applied for exemption under sub-section 
(2A) of Section 17 of the Employees’ Provident Funds and 
Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 <19 of 1952) (hereinafter 
referred to as the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that the 
employees of the said establishment arc, without making any 
separate contribution or payment of premium, in enjoyment 
of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme of the life 
Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of the Life Insu¬ 
rance which are more favourable to such employees than the 
benefits admissible under the Employees’ Deposit Linked In¬ 
surance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter referred to ns the said 
Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercke of the powers confererd by 
sub-section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject 







4$68 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : DECEMBER 29, 1984/P AUSA 8 , 1906 Part II Sfc. 3(ii)i 


to the conditions specified in the Schedule annexed hereto, 
the Central Government hereby exempts the said establish¬ 
ment from the operation of all the provisions of tho said 

S.hemc for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Eund Commis¬ 
sioner, Delhi and maintain such accounts and provide such 
facilities for inspection, as the Central Government may 
direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as 
the Central Government may, front time to time, direct 
under clause fa) of sub-section 1.3A1 of section 17 of the 
said Act, within 13 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the 
Group Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, 
submission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be 
borne by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government and as and 
when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient feature* 
thereof, in the language of the majority of the employees. 

3. Whereas an employee, who Is already a member of the 
Employees' Provident Fund or the Provident Fund oi an estab¬ 
lishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in his 
establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him as 
a member of tho Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately, if the benefit® available to the employees under 
tho said Scheme are enhanced, so that the benefits available 
under the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employees than the benefits admissible under the said 
Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than the amount that 
would be payable had employee been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the legal 
heir/nominee of the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Delhi and where 
any amendment is likely to affect adversely the interest of 
the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable opportu¬ 
nity to the employees to explain their point of view. 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as alieady 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled, 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insur¬ 
ance Corporation of India, and the policy Is allowed to lapse, 
the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer In 
payment of premium and responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of 
deceased members who would have been covered under the 
said Scbetne but for grant of this exemption, shall be that 
of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt payment of the sum assured to the nominee/legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in any 
rase within one month from the receipt of claim complete 
In all respects,

S.O. 4755.—Whereas Messrs Bennett Coleman and Company 
Limited, 7, Bahadur bhah Zafar Marg, New Delhi-2 (DL-125) 
thereinafter referred to as the said cstablistanent) have applied 
for exemption under sub-section (2A) of Section 17 of the 
'employees’ Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions 
Act, 1952 (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred to as the said Act); 

And whereas, the Central Government is satisfied that the 
employees of the yaid establishment are, without making any 
eparate contribution or payment of premium, in enjoyment 
/t benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme of the Life 
Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of Life Insurance 
which are more favourable to such employees than the benefits 
dmissible under the Employees’ Deposit Linked Insurance 
Scheme, 1976 (hcreinfate r referred to as the said Scheme); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub¬ 
jection (2A) of section 17 of thesaid Act and subject to the 
conditions specified in the schedule annexed hereto, the 
Central Government hereby exempts the said establishment 
from the operation of all the provisions of the said Scheme 
for a period of three years. 

SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner, Delhi and maintain such accounts and provide such 
facilities for inspection as the Central Government may direct 
from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pav such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of section 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved In the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer of 
accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 


Scheme as approved by the Central Government and as and 
when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient features 
thereof, in the language of the majority of the employees, 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already of the Employees’ 
Provident Fund or the Fund of an establishment exempted 
under the said Act, is employed in his establishment, the 
employer shall immediately enrol him as a member of the 
Group Insurance Scheme and pay necessary premium in res¬ 
pect of him to the Life Insurance Corporation of India. 

6. The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits avail¬ 
able to the employees under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately, if the benefits available to Ihe employees under 
the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the benefits available 
under the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employees than the benefits admissible under the said 
Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this a:heme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered under the said Scheme, 
the employer shall pay the dilferencs to the legal heir/nominee 
of the employee as compensation, 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Delhi and where 
any amendment is likely to affect adversely the interest of 
the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable opportu¬ 
nity to the employee to explain their point of view. 

9. Where, fo r any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the em¬ 
ployees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, the 
exemption shall be liable to.be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to nay the 
premium etc, within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to lapse, 
tha exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in pay¬ 
ment of premium and responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of deceased mem¬ 
bers who would have been covered under the said Scheme 
but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt payment of the sum assured to the nominec/Legal 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for it and in any case 
within one month from, the receipt of claim complete in all 
respect.

before giving his approval, give a reasonable opportunity to 
:ho employees to explain their point of view, 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Jcheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
idopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the em- 
rloyecs under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, the 
exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10 . Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insurance 
Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to lapse, the 
exemption is liable to be cancelled.

nent of premium and responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominees or the legal heirs of deceased members 
vho would have been covered under the said Scheme but for 
pant of this exemption, shall he that of the employer.

ichemc the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure

S.O. 4757.—Whereas Messrs, Saha and Rai Travels Private 
Limited, 9-A, Connaught Place, Middle Circle, New Delhi 
(DL-1556) (hereinafter referred to as the said establishment) 
have applied for exemption under sub-section (2A) of Section 



457? 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : pECEMBER 29,1984/PAUSA 8, lp(>6 


17 of the Employees’ Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Pro¬ 
visions Act, 1952 (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred to as the 
said Act); 


And whereas, the Central Government is aatified that the 
employees of the said establishment are without making any 
separate contribution or payment of premium, in enjoyment 
of benefits under the Group Insurance Scheme of the. Life 
Insurance Corporation of India in the nature of Life 
Insurance which are more favourable to such employees than 
the benefits admissible under the Employees’ Deposit Linked 
Insurance Scheme, 1976 (hereinafter referred to as the said 
Scheme); 


Now, therefore* in exercise of the powers conferred by sub- 
section (2A) of section 17 of the said Act and subject to the 
conditions specified in the schedule annexed hereto, the Central 
Government hereby exempts the said establishment from the 
operation of all the provisions of the said .Scheme for a 
period of three years. 


SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation to the said establishment shall 
submit such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commis- 
sinoer, Delhi and maintain such account j and provide such 
facilities for inspection as the Central Government may direct 
from time to time, 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A)of section 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of eve;/ month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns', payment of insurance premia, transfer of 
accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Oroup Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the C 'rural Government and as and 
when amended, alongwith a translation of the salient features 
thereof, in the language of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is abcady a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment, the employer shall immediately enrol him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces- 


, [Part II—Sbc. 3(ii)] 


ear? premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

. 6. The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits avail¬ 
able to the employees under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately, if the benefits available to the employees- under 
the said Scheme are enhanced, so that the benefits available 
under the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employees than the benefits admissible under the said 
Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amount 
payable under this scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had employee been covered undeT the said Scheme, 
the employer shall pay the difference to the legal heir/nonvnee 
of the employee as compensation. 

8 . No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insurance 
Scheme, shall be made without the prior approval of tht 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, Delhi and where any 
amendment is likely to affect adversely the interest of the 
employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner shall 
before giving his approval, give a reasonable opportunity to 
the employees to explain their point of view 

9. Where, for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the em¬ 
ployees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, the 
exemption shall be liable- to be cancelled. 

10. Where, for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to lapse, 
the exemption is liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in pay¬ 
ment of premium and responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominees o r the legal heirs of deceased members 
who would have been covered under the said Scheme but for 
grant of this exemption shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the members covered under the 
Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of India shall ensure 
prompt payment of the sum assticd to the nominee/Lefial 
heirs of the deceased member entitled for K and in any case 
within one month from the receipt of claim complete in all 
respects”.
